Learning with Bilevel-Minimax Optimization for Efficient and Reliable Transfer Attacks

使用双层极小极大优化进行学习，实现高效可靠的传输攻击

Yaohua Liu、Yifan Guo、Jiaxin Gao

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 ⭐ GitHub 开源

Transfer-based adversarial attacks craft adversarial examples using surrogate models to mislead black-box victim models. Beyond perturbation generation, transferability is fundamentally governed by the coupling of initialization, surrogate adaptation, and gradient dynamics. We revisit this challenge from a bilevel-minimax perspective and propose BMAT (Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer). The bilevel formulation captures the dependency between initialization and perturbation, while the inner minimax problem promotes surrogate robustness for cross-architecture generalization. Algorithmically, we develop an integrated bottom-up solver that combines a Soft Weight Modulator and an Implicit Gradient Approximator to enable ternary coupling among initialization, surrogate adaptation, and perturbation optimization. We further provide theoretical insights into the optimization dynamics of the proposed bilevel-minimax framework. Extensive experiments on classification and segmentation benchmarks show that BMAT outperforms more than 10 strong baselines across more than 30 victim models, improving both intra- and cross-architecture transfer and yielding up to a 2x reduction in mIoU. Code is available at https://github.com/callous-youth/BMAT.

基于转移的对抗性攻击使用代理模型来制作对抗性示例来误导黑盒受害者模型。除了扰动生成之外，可传递性从根本上受初始化、替代适应和梯度动力学的耦合控制。我们从双层极小极大的角度重新审视这一挑战，并提出了 BMAT（双层极小极大对抗转移）。双层公式捕获了初始化和扰动之间的依赖性，而内部极小极大问题则提高了跨架构泛化的代理鲁棒性。在算法上，我们开发了一个集成的自下而上求解器，它结合了软权重调制器和隐式梯度近似器，以实现初始化、代理适应和扰动优化之间的三元耦合。我们进一步提供了对所提出的双层极小极大框架的优化动力学的理论见解。关于分类和分割基准的大量实验表明，BMAT 在 30 多个受害者模型中优于 10 多个强基线，改善了架构内和跨架构传输，并将 mIoU 降低了 2 倍。代码可在 https://github.com/callous-youth/BMAT 获取。

Policy-Induced Hand Priors in Humanoid Dual-Arm Manipulation: Diagnosing and Mitigating Initial-Pose Dependence

人形双臂操作中策略诱导的手部先验：诊断和减轻初始姿势依赖性

Chaeyeon Jung、Juyoun Park

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 暂无开源

Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are expected to operate robustly across variations in the robot's initial configuration, yet aggregate task success can conceal pose-specific failures and inappropriate hand selection. This work investigates initial-pose dependence in VLA-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation. We characterize the initial-condition-dependent early hand preference as a policy-induced hand prior and quantify it using HandPriorScore, residual hand bias, and target responsiveness. Evaluations across multiple policies and 17 initial configurations reveal strong initial-pose--policy interactions: the same pose produces substantially different success rates across policies, while a single policy exhibits large performance variation across poses. Specific initial arm configurations can suppress or induce an asymmetric hand preference, with the resulting effect varying in direction and strength across policies. Wrist-camera observations also influence hand selection and task performance. Expanding initial-pose coverage in the training dataset substantially improves robustness, while targeted augmentation around a low-performing configuration increases its success rate. Comparisons across training configurations show that sufficient exposure to the target simulation task is beneficial, whereas the effect of real or auxiliary data depends on pose coverage, simulation ratio, and observation availability. These findings characterize a pose-conditioned hand prior, identify a localized initial arm configuration as a causal handle on hand-selection behavior, and demonstrate how data coverage and training composition affect initial-pose robustness.

视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略预计能够在机器人初始配置的变化中稳健运行，但总体任务成功可能会掩盖特定姿势的失败和不适当的手部选择。 This work investigates initial-pose dependence in VLA-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation.我们将依赖于初始条件的早期手牌偏好描述为策略诱导的手牌先验，并使用 HandPriorScore、残余手牌偏差和目标响应性对其进行量化。对多个策略和 17 种初始配置的评估揭示了强烈的初始姿势-策略相互作用：相同的姿势在不同策略中产生显着不同的成功率，而单个策略在不同姿势之间表现出巨大的性能差异。特定的初始手臂配置可以抑制或诱发不对称的手部偏好，从而导致不同策略的方向和强度各不相同。 Wrist-camera observations also influence hand selection and task performance.扩大训练数据集中的初始姿势覆盖范围可以显着提高鲁棒性，而围绕低性能配置的有针对性的增强则可以提高其成功率。不同训练配置的比较表明，充分接触目标模拟任务是有益的，而真实或辅助数据的效果取决于姿态覆盖、模拟比率和观察可用性。这些发现先验地描述了姿势条件手的特征，将局部初始手臂配置确定为手选择行为的因果处理，并演示了数据覆盖和训练组成如何影响初始姿势的鲁棒性。

Towards the Harness of Embodied Agents

迈向实体代理的利用

Qi Wang、Tianyi Wang、Chengyang Li、Shikun Ban、Yurun Chen 等

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 暂无开源

The success of coding agents has established the harness as a paradigm: what an agent achieves depends not on the model alone, but on the infrastructure around it. We ask whether the same paradigm extends to embodied agents in the physical world. We present Thea, a harness in which an agentic loop orchestrates robot capabilities, each wrapped as a callable tool. It inherits the core components of coding agents, modified as the physical world requires. The world, however, withholds two abilities that software grants for free: reading the state of the world, and judging the outcome of an action. To bridge these gaps, Thea introduces Scene Graph as Context, a persistent, symbolic representation of the world, and Evaluation as Exit Codes, which detects when an action should terminate, judges whether it succeeded, and on failure diagnoses the cause. Together they close the loop between the agent and the physical world. Rich behaviors then emerge from the composition of tools, and the closed loop carries long-horizon tasks to completion in real environments.

编码代理的成功已经建立了一种范式：代理所实现的目标不仅取决于模型，还取决于模型周围的基础设施。我们询问同样的范式是否也适用于物理世界中的具体主体。我们推出了 Thea，一种由代理循环协调机器人功能的工具，每个功能都包装为可调用的工具。它继承了编码代理的核心组件，并根据物理世界的需要进行了修改。然而，这个世界却保留了软件免费提供的两种能力：解读世界的状态和判断行动的结果。为了弥补这些差距，Thea 引入了场景图（Scene Graph）作为上下文，这是世界的持久的、符号化的表示，以及评估（Evaluation）作为退出代码，它检测一个动作何时应该终止，判断它是否成功，并在失败时诊断原因。它们共同闭合了智能体和物理世界之间的循环。工具的组合会产生丰富的行为，闭环承载着长期任务在现实环境中完成。

Energy-Aware Wind-Resilient Routing for Truck-Assisted Multi-UAV Delivery under Wind Uncertainty

风力不确定性下卡车辅助多无人机交付的能量感知抗风路由

Tianshun Li、Yanggang Sheng、Hongliang Lu、Zhongzhen Wang、Haoang Li 等

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 暂无开源

Energy feasibility under wind uncertainty is a critical safety issue for low-altitude air-ground delivery. In truck-UAV systems, UAVs complete assigned deliveries and safely return to a mobile truck or depot, while wind-induced propulsion costs vary online and are only partially observable. Existing routing methods often rely on static or deterministic energy models, which may underestimate headwind, crosswind, battery-voltage, and return-feasibility risks. This paper proposes Energy-Aware Wind-Resilient Routing (EWR), an online risk-sensitive planning framework for wind-aware and energy-safe UAV routing. The delivery environment is represented as a time-dependent directed energy graph whose edge costs are updated using delayed noisy wind estimates, payload states, and conservative uncertainty margins. Experiments using synthetic delivery graphs with replayed wind logs from a public truck-UAV delivery dataset show that EWR improves mission success rates and reduces wind-induced return failures.

风不确定性下的能源可行性是低空空地输送的关键安全问题。在卡车无人机系统中，无人机完成指定的交付并安全返回移动卡车或仓库，而风引起的推进成本在线变化并且只能部分观察。现有的路线选择方法通常依赖于静态或确定性能量模型，这可能会低估逆风、侧风、电池电压和返回可行性风险。本文提出了能源感知风弹性路由（EWR），这是一种用于风感知和能源安全无人机路由的在线风险敏感规划框架。交付环境表示为依赖于时间的有向能量图，其边缘成本使用延迟的噪声风估计、有效载荷状态和保守的不确定性裕度进行更新。使用合成交付图和公共卡车无人机交付数据集中重播的风日志进行的实验表明，EWR 提高了任务成功率并减少了风引起的返回失败。

Self-Evolving Embodied Agents via Skill-Harness Evolution

通过技能进化实现自我进化的具体代理

Peidong Wang、Zhiming Ma、Ying Chang、Xufang Luo、Xiaocui Yang 等

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 暂无开源

Embodied agents are increasingly built as systems around foundation models, where performance depends not only on model weights but also on the skills, context, action interfaces, and execution harness surrounding the model. While supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning can adapt agents to new environments, they require additional data, rewards, and training runs; meanwhile, many train-free code-centric approaches rely on programmable robot APIs that may be unavailable in fixed-interface settings. We propose SHAPER, a self-evolving framework for train-free embodied adaptation that keeps model parameters frozen and improves the non-parametric agent system by evolving reusable skills and a context-code harness through target-environment rollouts. In SHAPER, the same frozen model can serve as both planner and optimizer, refining its external skills and context-code harness without parameter updates. We evaluate SHAPER on VLABench and ESI-Bench, covering embodied agents with different low-level action interfaces, and compare against pure execution, supervised fine-tuning, and test-time-scaling baselines such as verifier-free selection and voting. Our results suggest that skill-and-harness optimization is a practical route to self-evolving embodied agents when model training is expensive, unavailable, or undesirable.

实体代理越来越多地构建为围绕基础模型的系统，其中性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于模型周围的技能、上下文、操作接口和执行工具。虽然监督微调和强化学习可以使智能体适应新环境，但它们需要额外的数据、奖励和训练运行；与此同时，许多免训练的以代码为中心的方法依赖于可编程机器人 API，而这些 API 在固定接口设置中可能不可用。我们提出了 SHAPER，这是一种用于免训练体现适应的自我进化框架，它可以保持模型参数冻结，并通过目标环境推出来发展可重用技能和上下文代码利用来改进非参数代理系统。在 SHAPER 中，相同的冻结模型可以充当规划器和优化器，从而改进其外部技能和上下文代码利用，而无需更新参数。我们在 VLABench 和 ESI-Bench 上评估 SHAPER，涵盖具有不同低级操作接口的具体代理，并与纯执行、监督微调和测试时间缩放基线（例如无验证者选择和投票）进行比较。我们的结果表明，当模型训练昂贵、不可用或不受欢迎时，技能和装备优化是实现自我进化的具体代理的实用途径。

Synchronizing Beliefs with Second-Order Theory-of-Mind in Human-Autonomy Teams (Extended Version)

在人类自主团队中将信念与二阶心理理论同步（扩展版）

Jack Mirenzi、Henny Admoni

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 暂无开源

Comparative feedback, asking people which of two behaviors they prefer, has become a standard way to align robot and agent behavior with human intent when the reward itself cannot be specified directly. Preference-based reward learning typically casts the human teacher as a passive oracle answering learner-generated queries. We argue this forfeits the teacher's defining advantage: knowledge of the objective. A teacher who knows the target can construct training examples more efficiently than any learner-driven acquisition strategy, an advantage that widens as the reward's feature dimension grows. However, exploiting this advantage requires an accurate model of what the learner currently knows. We therefore recast preference learning as a human-autonomy team problem coupling two behavioral models: the teacher maintains a model of the learner to design an informative curriculum, and the learner maintains a second-order model of the teacher's model, emitting structured preference constraints (understanding statements) that keep the teacher's model of the learner synchronized. In simulation, an informed teacher outperforms learner-led selection; teacher-model drift under alternating teachers erodes this advantage; and understanding statements repair it, with second-order (ToM-2) statements outperforming mean-belief statements when the teacher's error about the learner is concentrated in a particular direction rather than spread evenly.

当奖励本身无法直接指定时，比较反馈（询问人们更喜欢两种行为中的哪一种）已成为使机器人和代理行为与人类意图保持一致的标准方法。基于偏好的奖励学习通常将人类教师视为回答学习者生成的查询的被动预言家。我们认为这丧失了教师的决定性优势：对目标的了解。了解目标的教师可以比任何学习者驱动的习得策略更有效地构建训练示例，这一优势随着奖励特征维度的增长而扩大。然而，利用这一优势需要学习者当前所知道的内容的准确模型。因此，我们将偏好学习重新定义为一个耦合两种行为模型的人类自主团队问题：教师维护学习者模型来设计信息丰富的课程，学习者维护教师模型的二阶模型，发出结构化偏好约束（理解语句），使教师的学习者模型保持同步。在模拟中，消息灵通的教师胜过学习者主导的选择；教师交替下的教师模型漂移削弱了这一优势；当教师对学习者的错误集中在一个特定方向而不是均匀分布时，理解陈述可以修复它，二阶（ToM-2）陈述优于平均信念陈述。

A Browser-Based Gesture-Driven Avatar Interaction Framework for Metaverse Onboarding Environments

适用于 Metaverse 入门环境的基于浏览器的手势驱动头像交互框架

Deepti Parachuri、Chhayank Sahu、Sameer Singh Choudhary

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 暂无开源

Avatar interaction shapes how engaging and immersive a metaverse experience feels, and for that interaction to feel natural, avatars need to respond to users without forcing them through a controller-based interface first. This paper describes a gesture-driven interaction layer built for a browser-based metaverse onboarding environment, where users explore a set of virtual rooms as an avatar and interact with embedded video, document, and quiz content using hand, arm, and head gestures instead of a keyboard or controller. The system combines real-time gesture recognition (Google MediaPipe) with two alternative locomotion techniques - hand-raise navigation and in-place walking - so users can trade off precision against physical immersion depending on the task. The contribution is the integration, deployment, and evaluation of these techniques as a single lightweight, web-deployable, controller-free interaction model, assessed through a structured internal onboarding session with five participants. We report what worked, what didn't, and the design trade-offs that came out of combining these techniques in one deployed system.

化身交互塑造了元宇宙体验的吸引力和沉浸感，为了让交互感觉自然，化身需要对用户做出响应，而不是强迫他们首先通过基于控制器的界面。本文描述了为基于浏览器的 Metaverse 入门环境构建的手势驱动交互层，用户可以在其中以化身的身份探索一组虚拟房间，并使用手、手臂和头部手势（而不是键盘或控制器）与嵌入式视频、文档和测验内容进行交互。该系统将实时手势识别（Google MediaPipe）与两种替代运动技术（举手导航和原地行走）相结合，因此用户可以根据任务在精度和物理沉浸之间进行权衡。贡献在于将这些技术集成、部署和评估为单个轻量级、可通过 Web 部署、无控制器的交互模型，并通过五位参与者的结构化内部入职会议进行评估。我们报告什么有效，什么无效，以及将这些技术组合到一个已部署系统中所产生的设计权衡。

目标检测/分割生成模型/扩散3D视觉视频理解

MVFM-3DAD: Multi-view Flow Matching for 3D Anomaly Detection via Density Proxy Estimation

MVFM-3DAD：通过密度代理估计进行 3D 异常检测的多视图流匹配

Liangwei Li、Lin Liu、Jing Zhang、Xiaohui Du、Ruqian Hao 等

论文页面 PDF HTML 版本 ⭐ GitHub 开源

In 3D anomaly detection (3DAD), most existing methods rely on Memory bank retrieval or reconstruction. However, memory-based methods are constrained by the coverage of stored normal features, while reconstruction-based methods may learn identity shortcuts that also reconstruct anomalous inputs well. These limitations motivate a density-oriented approach that evaluates whether a test sample follows the learned normal distribution. To this end, we propose MVFM-3DAD, a flow-based framework that reframes 3DAD as density proxy estimation over the normal data distribution. MVFM-3DAD introduces a Bidirectional Geometric Projector (BGP), whose forward process converts irregular point clouds into structured multi-view representations. The Flow-guided Density Proxy Estimator (FDPE) estimates a reference density for each view feature, after which the backward process of BGP maps these multi-view density estimates to their corresponding 3D points. Building on it, anomalous features can be identified by their terminal normality. Unlike conventional flow-based likelihood estimation, our formulation requires neither input reconstruction nor explicit Jacobian evaluation, yielding a simple and efficient anomaly-scoring mechanism. Extensive experiments show that MVFM-3DAD outperforms the strongest competing methods on Real3D-AD and MVTec3D-AD. Code is available at https://github.com/lil-wayne-0319/MV3D-AD

在 3D 异常检测 (3DAD) 中，大多数现有方法依赖于记忆库检索或重建。然而，基于记忆的方法受到存储的正常特征的覆盖范围的限制，而基于重建的方法可以学习也可以很好地重建异常输入的身份快捷方式。这些限制激发了一种面向密度的方法，该方法评估测试样本是否遵循学习的正态分布。为此，我们提出了 MVFM-3DAD，这是一种基于流的框架，它将 3DAD 重新构建为正态数据分布上的密度代理估计。 MVFM-3DAD 引入了双向几何投影仪 (BGP)，其前向过程将不规则点云转换为结构化多视图表示。流引导密度代理估计器 (FDPE) 估计每个视图特征的参考密度，之后 BGP 的后向过程将这些多视图密度估计映射到其相应的 3D 点。在此基础上，异常特征可以通过其最终常态来识别。与传统的基于流的似然估计不同，我们的公式既不需要输入重建，也不需要显式的雅可比评估，从而产生了简单而有效的异常评分机制。大量实验表明，MVFM-3DAD 的性能优于 Real3D-AD 和 MVTec3D-AD 上最强的竞争方法。代码可在 https://github.com/lil-wayne-0319/MV3D-AD 获取
